Here are the key findings:\n\n## World Partition Streaming Changes and Actor Operations\n\nData Layers is a system within World Partition you can use to organize your Actors both in the Editor and at runtime. Using Data Layer Assets and Data Layer Instances, you can dynamically load and unload layers in the Editor to help manage complex worlds.\n\n### Non-Spatially-Loaded Actors\n\nNon-spatially-loaded actors on runtime data layers will still get extracted to separate streaming levels and will no longer provide any guarantees about loading behavior since they stream in like everything else.\n\n### Runtime Cell Transformers\n\nRuntime Cell Transformers are used to apply additional transformations to the generation of streaming levels. These are classes with simple \"(Pre/Post)Transform\" methods that can be added to world settings - every streaming level generated is fed into the transform methods and the transformer class can opt to modify the level or run any other operation you want. Runtime cell transformers do not operate at runtime: they run only when streaming generation runs in the editor (entering PIE, cooking, etc). They modify generated runtime cells, hence the name.\n\n### Level Instancing with World Partition\n\nLevel Instances and Packed Level Blueprints support World Partition: Data Layers. The Actors contained within the Level Instance inherit the Data Layer assigned to its Level Instance Actor by default. Actors within the Level Instance also support additional Data Layers.\n\nThe search results provide documentation from UE 5.7 official sources regarding these specific aspects of World Partition's actor operations and streaming behavior.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"3f4130f3-3f2b-48a2-b6b8-d553f018ae79","timestamp":"2026-02-18T16:24:25.640Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4ca724ac-47e4-4265-8dc6-f62f06f8d93a"}

It's comprised of three integral systems: Nanite Assemblies, which manage the foliage part instances into one cohesive unit; Nanite Skinning, which determines the dynamic behavior it will have with systems like wind; and Nanite Voxels, which are pixel sized voxels that are able to retain details, animation and material characteristics based on the distance from the camera.\n\n## Landscape Grass Types\n\nThe Procedural Vegetation Editor supports three mesh output types: Static Mesh for small plants, bushes, or background details where animation isn't needed; Nanite Static Mesh for forests, jungles, or close-up plants where detail matters; and Nanite Skeletal Mesh for dense forests with detail and wind animations where you need both realism and movement.\n\n## API and Customization\n\nThe Procedural Vegetation Editor relies on using preset assets, called Megaplants, that contain data to build specific species of vegetation, with preset assets containing Skeletal Meshes, Materials and Textures, with nanite foliage support for multiple variations to create realistic vegetation in your project.\n\nThe search results don't provide specific information about API-level changes for modifying grass types or landscape grass properties in UE 5.7. Unreal Engine can export your heightmap file as a 16-bit grayscale .png or a 16-bit grayscale .r16 file with a variety of supported options.\n\n## Technical Specifications\n\nTo import the heights for a Landscape, you need a heightmap of 631x631 vertices, because there is always one more row of vertices than there are quads. Unreal Engine calculates your heightmap's height by using values between -256 and 255.992, stored with 16-bit precision, and the calculated height is then multiplied by the Z scale value that you input when you import the heightmap data.\n\n## Custom Formats\n\nLandscape layers can be imported from external programs via implementation of the ILandscapeHeightmapFileFormat and ILandscapeWeightmapFileFormat interfaces.\n\nFor programmatic C++ landscape creation with LandscapeEditorUtils, the search results reference using the helper class FHeightmapAccessor to write custom heightmap data to the Landscape, though more specific documentation about UE 5.7's LandscapeEditorUtils would require additional resources.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"e22478b8-9365-4c6b-93f8-92d485f9ab04","timestamp":"2026-02-18T16:29:03.337Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e7373dbb-c0f7-4472-ae60-0332df29f05b"}

and for the graph to be generated from a PCG component. To enable PCG graph standalone execution, you can enable a new Is Standalone Graph option is available in each graph's settings. Click the Cog icon next to the Trash button, and a new details panel will open showing all configurable settings for the edit layer type.\n\n### Specialized Edit Layers\nEdit layers are designed to be multifunctional, with certain Landscape systems using specialized edit layers to apply procedural, feature specific modifications to the terrain, which are automatically managed and provide structured, non-destructive control over specific features.\n\nTwo specialized layer types include:\n- Splines Edit Layer: Landscape splines data can now be created and managed in a separate, non destructive layer, allowing you to edit, change, and move splines non-destructively with the landscape terraforming automatically.\n- Patches Edit Layer: Landscape patch data can now be created and managed in a separate, non destructive layer, allowing you to edit, change, and move patches non-destructively with the landscape terraforming automatically.\n\n### Paint Layer System\nWeight-Blended layers are the most commonly used type of layer info object, where painting a weight-blended layer will decrease the weight of all other weight-blended layers (for example, painting mud will remove grass). Non Weight-Blended layers are independent of each other, and these are used for more advanced effects, such as blending snow onto other layers.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"98dc9059-b978-4f19-b923-7db3bcc7b0dc","timestamp":"2026-02-18T16:33:12.500Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5b45477a-8ad8-45cf-9fc3-8523c7db141c"}

{"parentUuid":"529c60cc-8448-4a47-a965-9df74b4e574d","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/mnt/d/tempofresh/TempoSample/Plugins/AgentBridge","sessionId":"273719e1-a3ed-433f-85fc-d301ef7a8742","version":"2.1.45","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a69b8c5","slug":"abstract-tumbling-conway","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01VJ4ZBT99JKjAYZ4xsc8v5g","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now I have comprehensive data. Let me compile the findings into a detailed report organized by subsystem.\n\n---\n\n# UE 5.7 Changes Research Report\n\nThis report covers Unreal Engine 5.7 changes that could affect the AgentBridge plugin, organized by subsystem. UE 5.7 was released in late 2025 (approximately November/December 2025).\n\n**Important caveat:** Epic Games does not publish a comprehensive C++ API diff document between minor versions. The UE 5.7 release notes focus on feature announcements, not low-level API changes. Some findings below are inferred from documentation changes, forum reports, and feature descriptions. The most reliable way to identify breaking changes for your specific codebase is to compile against UE 5.7 and address errors.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Build System Changes (BREAKING)\n\n### C++17 is Obsolete - Must Use C++20\n\n**Severity: BREAKING for any `.Build.cs` that explicitly sets `CppStandardVersion.Cpp17`**\n\nUE 5.7 has marked `CppStandardVersion.Cpp17` as obsolete with the error: \"C++17 is no longer allowed, code will not compile with this version.\" Any `.Build.cs` file that explicitly sets `CppStandard = CppStandardVersion.Cpp17` must be updated to `CppStandardVersion.Cpp20` or removed (UE 5.7 defaults to C++20).\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** Check all 4 `.Build.cs` files. If none explicitly set a C++ standard, this is a non-issue (UE defaults to C++20). If any set C++17 explicitly, they will fail to compile.\n\n### MSVC 14.44 Preferred Compiler\n\n**Severity: Low (additive)**\n\nThe preferred MSVC compiler version has been updated from 14.38 (UE 5.5/5.6) to 14.44. This is configured in `Engine/Config/Windows/Windows_SDK.json`. Developers should have Visual Studio 2022 with the 14.44 toolset installed.\n\n### SDL2 to SDL3 Transition (Linux Only)\n\n**Severity: BREAKING for Linux builds that reference SDL2**\n\nAny `.Build.cs` referencing the `SDL2` module must change to `SDL3`. SDL3 header files are now under `SDL3/` subdir. SDL3 API return values changed -- functions now return success/failure booleans instead of negative values.\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** Likely no impact unless any module directly references SDL. gRPC integration uses its own networking, not SDL.\n\nSources:\n- [Fix for RiderLink 5.7 - CppStandardVersion.Cpp17 obsolete](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/fix-for-riderlink-5-7-error-riderlink-plugin-fails-to-build-on-ue5-main-cppstandardversion-cpp17-obsolete/2684868)\n- [Updating UE on Linux to SDL3](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/updating-unreal-engine-on-linux-to-sdl3)\n- [Building Unreal with MSVC 14.42/14.44](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/building-unreal-with-msvc-14-42-14-44/2609153)\n- [JetBrains/UnrealLink Issue #291](https://github.com/JetBrains/UnrealLink/issues/291)\n\n---\n\n## 2. Landscape System Changes (MIXED - Additive with Regressions)\n\n### New ALandscapeProxy Functions (Additive)\n\nUE 5.7 adds several new functions to `ALandscapeProxy`:\n\n| New Function | Purpose |\n|---|---|\n| `CreateLandscapeTextureArray()` | Creates a `Texture2DArray` for landscape proxy or components |\n| `CreateLandscapeToolTexture()` | Creates textures for tool usage |\n| `AddTargetLayer()` | Adds a target layer (with optional unique name) |\n| `RemoveTargetLayer()` | Removes named target layers |\n| `UpdateTargetLayer()` | Updates existing target layer settings |\n| `GetTargetLayers()` | Retrieves all target layers |\n| `SetLandscapeMaterialScalarParameterValue()` | Sets float params on all landscape components |\n| `SetLandscapeMaterialTextureParameterValue()` | Sets texture params on all components |\n| `SetLandscapeMaterialVectorParameterValue()` | Sets vector/color params |\n\n**Impact on LANDSCAPE_OPS plan:** The new `AddTargetLayer()` / `RemoveTargetLayer()` / `GetTargetLayers()` functions could simplify the layer management code in the `PaintLandscapeLayers` implementation. The plan currently uses `FAlphamapAccessor` for direct weight painting -- this approach should still work, but the new target layer functions provide a higher-level API worth investigating.\n\n### New Landscape Edit Layer Types (Additive)\n\nUE 5.7 introduces specialized edit layers:\n- **Splines Edit Layer** -- Non-destructive landscape spline management\n- **Patches Edit Layer** -- Non-destructive landscape patch management\n- **Edit Layer Inspector Panel** -- New UI for configuring edit layer settings\n\nThese are new features that don't break existing code but may change how edit layers interact.\n\n### Landscape Paint Weight Blending Regression (BUG)\n\n**Severity: HIGH for `PaintLandscapeLayers` implementation**\n\nMultiple users report that landscape weight blending behavior changed in UE 5.7:\n- Paint brush now uses only half of its weight alpha (was full previously)\n- Edit layer ordering affects weight: top layer gets full weight, but painting doesn't erase from bottom layers\n- Some layers can only paint onto base layer, failing to paint over other layers\n\nThis is a known regression that affects programmatic weight painting (our `FAlphamapAccessor` approach). The LANDSCAPE_OPS plan's elevation-based auto-painting may produce different visual results on UE 5.7 vs 5.6.\n\n### Landscape Grass Density Change (BUG)\n\nLandscape grass appears significantly sparser in 5.7 compared to 5.5.4. This is a visual regression but does not affect the AgentBridge API directly.\n\n### Virtual Texture Collections Shader Errors (BUG)\n\nVirtual texture collections have introduced breaking shader compilation issues in 5.7 preview, with \"unknown type name 'FResourceCollection'\" errors in `Material.ush`. If landscape materials use virtual textures, they may encounter shader compilation failures.\n\n### Core Import API (Likely Unchanged)\n\nThe key function signatures documented in the LANDSCAPE_OPS plan appear to still exist in UE 5.7:\n- `ALandscapeProxy::Import()` -- Still documented in UE 5.7 API reference\n- `EditorSetLandscapeMaterial()` -- Still present\n- `LandscapeImportHeightmapFromRenderTarget()` / `LandscapeExportHeightmapToRenderTarget()` -- Still documented\n\n**However, the exact function signatures should be verified against 5.7 headers before implementation.** The plan was validated against UE 5.6 source. Function parameter lists may have gained additional parameters (especially given the new edit layer system).\n\nSources:\n- [ALandscapeProxy UE 5.7 Documentation](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/API/Runtime/Landscape/ALandscapeProxy)\n- [Landscape Edit Layers in UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/landscape-edit-layers-in-unreal-engine)\n- [Landscape Paint Weight Blending is broken](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/landscape-paint-weight-blending-is-broken/2673901)\n- [5.7 Landscape paint layer blend issue](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/5-7-landscape-paint-layer-blend-issue/2677415)\n- [UE 5.7 Problem with paint material on landscape](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/unreal-engine-5-7-problem-with-the-paint-material-on-the-landscape/2674846)\n- [UE 5.7 Preview Forum Thread](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/unreal-engine-5-7-preview/2658958)\n\n---\n\n## 3. PCG (Procedural Content Generation) Changes (SIGNIFICANT - Mostly Additive)\n\n### PCG Now Production-Ready (Status Change)\n\nPCG officially moved from Experimental/Beta to **Production-Ready** in UE 5.7. This means the API surface should be more stable going forward, but the transition itself may have involved cleanup/deprecation of experimental APIs.\n\n### Standalone Graph Execution (NEW)\n\n**Severity: Additive but architecturally significant**\n\nPCG graphs can now execute as standalone assets without a world context or `UPCGComponent`. A new `Is Standalone Graph` setting enables this. This enables asset creation workflows (meshes, textures, PCG Data Assets) from graph execution alone.\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** The existing `pcg_list_nodes`, `pcg_add_node`, `pcg_connect` tools operate on graph assets. Standalone execution could mean graphs behave differently without a world context. The current AgentBridge PCG tools should still work for editor-time graph editing, but any runtime execution assumptions may need updating.\n\n### Custom Data Types (NEW)\n\nUE 5.7 removes the hardcoded list of PCG data types. Plugins can now register custom data types within the type hierarchy. New built-in types include:\n- **Polygon2D** -- Closed areas convertible to surfaces or splines, with intersection, union, and difference operators\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** The `pcg_add_node` tool dispatches by node type string. New node types work automatically. No breaking change expected.\n\n### Biome Core V2 (Experimental, ARCHITECTURAL CHANGE)\n\n**Severity: MEDIUM for projects using PCG Biome**\n\nBiome Core V2 introduces significant architectural changes from V1:\n- Everything is now defined and generated **locally per Biome Actor** (new `Local Biome Core PCG Component`)\n- Local assets and default biome definition can be embedded directly per-actor (reduces separate asset file requirements)\n- New local preview mode for more efficient biome creation\n- Graph simplification: inline constants, removed unnecessary loops, reduced biome cache complexity\n- New GPU ground scatter example using PCG GPU landscape RVT interface\n\n**Key classes/structures:**\n- `BiomeDefinitionTemplate` -- Biome definitions (BiomeName, BiomeColor, BiomePriority)\n- `BiomeAssetBaseTemplate` -- Asset configs (Generator, Mesh, Assembly, Actor, ChildAssets)\n- `BiomeGeneratorTemplate` -- Generator definitions (GeneratorType, GeneratorPriority, GeneratorGraph)\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** The PCG biome workflow documented in AGENTS.md Phase 10 (which uses `NotifyPropertiesChangedFromBlueprint()` on `PCG_LocalBiomeCore`) may need updating if the component architecture changed. The \"Local Biome Core PCG Component\" naming matches what AgentBridge already targets, but property paths and component names should be verified.\n\n### FastGeo / Componentless Primitives (Experimental)\n\nPCG GPU now supports spawning primitives via FastGeo components instead of actor components. Enable via `PCGFastGeoInterop` plugin and CVar `pcg.RuntimeGeneration.ISM.ComponentlessPrimitives = 1`. These are simpler and more compact, requiring less game thread time.\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** No direct API impact. This affects how PCG-spawned meshes appear in the world -- they may not show up as regular actor components in `query_actors` since they bypass the actor/component system entirely.\n\n### PCG Performance (2x improvement)\n\nGPU and game-thread optimizations push PCG to nearly 2x performance vs UE 5.5. Parameter overrides now supported on GPU nodes.\n\n### New PCG Node Types (Additive)\n\n| New Node | Purpose |\n|---|---|\n| Spline Intersection | Intersect splines |\n| Split Splines | Split splines into segments |\n| Get Segment | Extract spline segments |\n| Extract Attribute | Extract attributes from points |\n| Export Selected Attributes | Export specific attributes |\n| Bitwise/Boolean operators | Logical operations |\n| Bounds from Mesh | Supports skeletal meshes now |\n| Scene Capture (GPU) | GPU scene capture |\n| Save Texture to Asset (GPU) | Save GPU textures as assets |\n| Execute Python Script | Python scripting in PCG graphs (new plugin) |\n| Cull Points (GPU) | GPU-accelerated culling |\n| Transform Points (GPU) | GPU-accelerated transforms |\n\n### Procedural Vegetation Editor (Experimental, NEW)\n\nNew plugin for creating/editing vegetation meshes with Nanite support, wind animation, voxelization. Built on PCG nodes. This is a separate plugin, not a change to core PCG.\n\nSources:\n- [PCG Overview UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/procedural-content-generation-overview)\n- [Explore Production-Ready PCG in UE 5.7](https://80.lv/articles/what-s-new-in-pcg-in-ue5-6-ue5-7)\n- [PCG Biome Core Reference Guide](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/procedural-content-generation-pcg-biome-core-and-sample-plugins-reference-guide-in-unreal-engine)\n- [Biome Core V2 on UE Roadmap](https://portal.productboard.com/epicgames/1-unreal-engine-public-roadmap/c/2015-biome-core-v2-plugin-experimental-)\n- [PCG Biome Core Overview](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/procedural-content-generation-pcg-biome-core-and-sample-plugins-overview-guide-in-unreal-engine)\n- [UE 5.7 PCG Updates Explained (Creative Bloq)](https://www.creativebloq.com/3d/video-game-design/the-unreal-engine-5-7-procedural-content-generation-update-explained)\n- [PCG GPU Processing UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/using-pcg-with-gpu-processing-in-unreal-engine)\n\n---\n\n## 4. Property System / Reflection (LIKELY UNCHANGED, Minor Deprecations)\n\n### FProperty Deprecations (Ongoing from earlier versions)\n\nThese deprecations originated in earlier UE5 versions but may now produce compile errors if `UE_DEPRECATED` warnings were previously just warnings:\n\n| Deprecated | Replacement | Since |\n|---|---|---|\n| `FProperty::ElementSize` (member) | `FProperty::GetElementSize()` (function) | ~UE 5.1+ |\n| `FProperty::ImportText()` (direct pointer overload) | `ImportText_Direct()` or `ImportText_InContainer()` | ~UE 5.1+ |\n| Compiled-in property constructor | Parameter struct constructors | ~UE 5.1+ |\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** The AgentBridgeCore module uses FProperty extensively for reflection. Check for usage of `ElementSize` member vs `GetElementSize()` method, and `ImportText()` vs `ImportText_Direct()`. These may have been warnings in 5.6 but could become errors in 5.7 if the deprecated versions were finally removed.\n\n### No Known Breaking Changes to Core Reflection\n\nThe core `FProperty`, `FStructProperty`, `FArrayProperty`, `UFunction` class hierarchy does not appear to have undergone structural changes in 5.7. Property paths, nested struct traversal, and array element access should continue to work as before.\n\nSources:\n- [FProperty UE 5.7 Documentation](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/API/Runtime/CoreUObject/FProperty)\n- [UE UProperties Documentation](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/unreal-engine-uproperties)\n\n---\n\n## 5. Actor Operations (NO KNOWN BREAKING CHANGES)\n\n### SpawnActor API\n\nThe `UWorld::SpawnActor()` and `UWorld::SpawnActorDeferred()` APIs appear unchanged in UE 5.7 documentation. The `FActorSpawnParameters` struct is still present and documented.\n\n### Missing Header Includes\n\nUE 5.7 preview revealed that some templates require explicit `#include \"Engine/World.h\"` where it was previously included transitively. This affected `PlatformingCharacter.cpp`, `SideScrollingNPC.cpp`, etc.\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** If AgentBridge code relies on transitive includes of `Engine/World.h`, it may need explicit includes. This is the most common type of \"breaking change\" in UE upgrades -- header dependency cleanup.\n\n### Blueprint Component Duplication Bug\n\nComponents cannot be duplicated, cut, copied, or pasted within Blueprint editors' Components window in 5.7 preview. This was expected to be fixed in 5.7.0 final release.\n\nSources:\n- [Spawning Actors UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/spawning-actors-in-unreal-engine)\n- [UWorld UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/API/Runtime/Engine/UWorld)\n- [UE 5.7 Preview Forum](https://forums.unrealengine.com/t/unreal-engine-5-7-preview/2658958)\n\n---\n\n## 6. Asset Operations (NO KNOWN BREAKING CHANGES)\n\n### FAssetRegistryModule / UAssetManager\n\nNo specific breaking changes documented for the asset registry or asset manager in UE 5.7. The `FAssetRegistryModule` still implements `IModuleInterface` and `IAssetRegistryInterface`. `UAssetManager::Get()` and `GetAssetRegistry()` remain available.\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** Asset creation, duplication, and saving operations should continue to work unchanged. Monitor for any transitive header changes during compilation.\n\nSources:\n- [Asset Registry UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/asset-registry-in-unreal-engine)\n- [FAssetRegistryModule UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/API/Runtime/AssetRegistry/FAssetRegistryModule)\n- [Asset Management UE 5.7](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/asset-management-in-unreal-engine)\n\n---\n\n## 7. World Partition / Streaming (MINOR ADDITIVE)\n\n### Custom HLOD Support (NEW)\n\nUE 5.7 adds a new `World Partition Custom HLOD` actor class allowing injection of custom representations or custom sources for HLOD generation. The HLOD setup UX has been reworked.\n\n**Impact on AgentBridge:** No breaking change. The existing `query_actors(include_unloaded=True)` and streaming state queries should continue to work. The Custom HLOD actors would appear as new actor types in queries.\n\n### No Core Streaming API Changes\n\nThe core World Partition streaming APIs (`UWorldPartitionStreamingSourceComponent`, data layers, streaming sources) do not appear to have changed in 5.7.\n\nSources:\n- [World Partition Roadmap](https://portal.productboard.com/epicgames/1-unreal-engine-public-roadmap/c/660-world-partition)\n- [UE 5.7 Release Notes](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/unreal-engine-5-7-release-notes)\n\n---\n\n## 8. gRPC Server Integration (NO DIRECT CHANGES)\n\nNo UE 5.7 changes specifically target gRPC integration. The Tempo plugin (which provides the gRPC transport) is separate from UE engine changes. The main risk is:\n\n1. **Header conflict evolution** -- If UE 5.7 changes transitive includes in headers like `AssetRegistry/AssetRegistryModule.h` or `Editor.h`, the existing header conflict isolation pattern (business logic in AgentBridgeScripting, not AgentBridgeServer) should continue to work but may need adjustments.\n\n2. **Windows SDK conflicts** -- gRPC's Windows SDK usage may interact differently with MSVC 14.44. Test the build early.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Networking / Iris Changes (NOT RELEVANT but Notable)\n\nUE 5.7 removes the `UReplicationBridge` base class and renames `BeginReplication` to `StartActorReplication`. Iris moved from Experimental to Beta. **This does not affect AgentBridge** unless the plugin uses Iris networking directly (it does not -- it uses Tempo's gRPC).\n\n---\n\n## Summary: Risk Assessment for AgentBridge\n\n| Subsystem | Risk Level | Action Required |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Build System (C++20)** | HIGH if C++17 is set | Check `.Build.cs` files for explicit `CppStandardVersion` |\n| **Landscape (paint blending)** | HIGH for LANDSCAPE_OPS plan | Test `FAlphamapAccessor` painting on 5.7;
